## Blocking conditions

**OD-03 — scope freeze.** Signed 25 August 2026; lapses 15 September 2026 if no
signed quotation lands. The amended quotation was signed on 4 September 2026
(recorded at OD-15 §2, `QUOTATION_AMENDMENTS.md`, CF-50). `SCOPE.md` still
derives from a signed quotation and is not authored. OD-03 is unamended;
whether the signature discharges its freeze role is a reviewer ruling.

**OD-04 — repository visibility.** Signed 25 August 2026. The repository is
PUBLIC during development, for review convenience, and reverts to PRIVATE
before production cutover. The revert is a G7 checklist item; until it is
done, G7 does not pass. Full conditions in `docs/DECISIONS.md` OD-04.

**OD-07 / OD-15 — quotation signed (CF-50 closed).** The amended quotation
carrying OD-07's brand refinement and OD-15's scope removal was signed on
4 September 2026. Both previously unfiled approvals are retired. CF-50 is
CLOSED at P05-T15B. `docs/research/09-locked-baseline.md` is the source for
branch and settings content.

**D-08 — two Operator accounts, attested 5 September 2026.** Two Operator accounts exist and both carry a verified TOTP factor. Recorded on the human's attestation, the same basis as CF-59 and CF-60 at G3 — an attestation by the row's owner, not a measurement by this project. `ADMIN_SPEC.md` §9 criterion 2 requires the auth state to be read at the gate, so G5 still closes criterion 2 by a read; this paragraph records that the second account now exists, not that the criterion has passed.

**M7B-2 lockout check, attested 7 September 2026.** On 7 September 2026 the human and the client each saved a draft through the deployed dashboard as a real Operator after M7B-2 applied, and both succeeded. Recorded as an attestation by the person who performed it, the same basis as CF-59, CF-60 and D-08 — not a measurement by this project. This task did not sign into any lasting Operator account.

Eleven paths, no directory, no pattern. `P05-T01c-F2` defines a checked box as done-and-certified, including a FAIL that a named fix repaired, and the phases guard now enforces that. `p05-t01b` and `p05-t01c` are on `main` as of `ddd6bfe`. `p05-t01c-f` and `p05-t01c-f2` are on `main` as of `a89ae32`.

## Phase map

| Order | Phase | Contents | Gate | Non-waivable |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| — | P00 | Prepare — docs, ODs, seed verified, clinical QA dispatch deferred (D-19) | G0 quotation signed, or OD-03 in force | — |
| — | P01 | Foundation — repo, CI, schema, RLS, seed import (Auth and MFA moved to P05 by OD-11) | G1 | Boundary |
| — | P02 | Design system — tokens, RTL primitives, lint rules | G2 — PASSED | — |
| 1 | P03 | Public site — 12 static + 1 dynamic route patterns, 42 rendered URLs, enumerated in `CONTENT_MODEL.md` §3c, both locales | G3 — PASSED | Boundary · Bilingual |
| 4 | P04 | Catalogue search — static index, bilingual aliases over Programmes and LabTests on the البرامج page — NOT the results portal, which is linked and never read (BOUNDARY_MODEL.md). **Gated on clinical sign-off (CF-22)**: no index artefact is emitted while the PR-08 flag is off | G4 | **Clinical** · Data integrity · Bilingual |
| 2 | P05 | Admin dashboard — 8 modules enumerated in `CONTENT_MODEL.md` §3d, Operator accounts, Auth and MFA (OD-11) | G5 — PASSED | Boundary · Bilingual |
| 3 | P06 | Content & Arabic — translation, entry, clinical sign-off | G6 | **Clinical** · Bilingual |
| 5 | P07 | Hardening & cutover — headers, DNS, redirects, decommission. **OD-04 revert to PRIVATE is a G7 checklist item** | G7 launch | **Clinical** · **Boundary** · Bilingual · Data integrity |
| — | P08 | PartnerLab accounts and private Offers (OD-15). Follows G5; position relative to G7 undecided | G8 | Boundary |

Build order is Phase-1-first — public site before dashboard — so that if the
launch date forces a split, the phasing already matches. OD-12 retains that
principle and extends it: delivery order is P03 → P05 → P06 → P04 → P07.
P08 follows G5; its position relative to G7 is undecided and is not in OD-12's sequence.
Phases keep their identifiers and their gates; only the order in which they
are reached changes.
